## Foil RESTAP endpoint built (user: "build it" re nxt3d/restap)
- ~/workspace/restap/server.mjs — zero-dep Node, localhost 127.0.0.1:1871. Endpoints: GET /.well-known/restap.json, POST /talk (async, queues to inbox), GET /news?session_id= (replies), POST /news (one-way, never replies).

## HivePunkz stock-reward research — confirmed payouts (2026-10-01)
- User surfaced https://opensea.io/collection/hivepunkz and asked whether anyone had actually cashed out stock rewards.
- HivePunkz is a 1,111-item Base collection by HivemindOS, contract `0x3a21403499e05dd5109bf5ad708b790bf436300d`. Official/OpenSea description: holders clock bees in with $HIVE to share a weekly pot of tokenized NVIDIA stock; 7.5% resale royalties go to the pot.
- Deep research verified live onchain that holders have received **NVDAc**, Coinbase’s B20 tokenized NVIDIA stock on Base (`0xb20000000000000000000078ee7ce2fE4908108C`), represented as 1:1 backed by NVDA held by Alpaca Securities and Reg S / unavailable to US persons.
- BaseScan showed multiple real claims from the distributor `0x52D6f84e42B7Bb155857C3EA54d7f24DF2D1f3f6` to roughly 10+ distinct wallets from Sep 27–30, 2026. Examples ranged from about $6.52 to about $58.40 in NVDAc. Pot funding was also visible: a Sep 28 transaction converted 32.458378 USDC into 0.14100934 NVDAc on Aerodrome, with several other buys Sep 26–28.
- Documented mechanics: 10 USDC mint, with 70% ($7) to the pot; one-time 5M $HIVE clock-in per bee (90% burned, 10% treasury); weekly split by rarity/form weight × clocked-in hours; keeper buys NVDAc once at least $25 accumulates; $7,777 launch seed spread over eight weeks; evolving can lock up to 100M HIVE for up to 3× weight.
- Market/risk caveats observed Oct 1: collection appeared fully minted; OpenSea showed $35 floor, about $484.93 total volume, 82 unique owners, 91 listed, and a 27.6% one-day floor decline. No third-party contract audit was submitted on BaseScan. Long-term rewards depend on royalties/fees after the front-loaded seed period, and the collection is thinly held/traded.
- No scam or controversy was found in the research, but public-sale distribution vs team-minted remainder could not be verified. No X/Discord payout announcements were found; payout confirmation rests on the onchain distributor and token-transfer records.
- Full researcher report available at `sandbox://workspace/research_notes/hivepunkz-stock-payouts-20261001-2020/report.md` if the user asks for raw notes.
